**Requests this segment (in order):**
- Fix the lift going below the bottom (done).
- TEMP rings 1/2: the claw opens unexpectedly. During transport the claw must stay at CLWC (arm_calib step 3). The user rejected speed limiting ("限制速度没必要").
- Ring-2 alignment: wheels only, no arm correction, larger correction range (done).
- Claw opening only two kinds: the raw-plate grab uses CLWOR; everywhere else uses the car-tray opening CLWOT (done).
- Deliberate adjustable offset when aligning ring 2, ±20mm, user-adjustable (done: `r2off`).
- Lift auto-sync after a NOENC boot (done).
- Raw area: no "抽搐" (keepalive twitch), fix not grabbing, faster and more accurate alignment. "一次性解决这三个问题不要再让我返工了".
- **LATEST:** "他对准的时间太长了还有对准了之后就应该立刻识别颜色然后夹取啊为什么要等待那么久还有就是这个夹子一会儿夹取一会儿不夹取不知道在干什么" (also "有两个下爪子了").

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- **Lift (Emm ID5, belt, encoder 40mm/rev):**
  - Lift_Check after moves (LIFTSLIP; Stop_Now; up to 15mm correction, or ±20mm when the height was guessed → LIFTSYNC).
  - LIFT_TOP_MM 130 as the boot wrap bound.
  - lift_nochk during CAL; Lift_Rebase0 on LIFT ZERO.
  - lift_guess after a NOENC boot; LIFT SYNC command; boot waits ~6s for the driver.
- **Claw (PWM, TIM2 CH3, PA2):**
  - Claw_Move(target, why) prints `CLAWSET <us> <why>`.
  - claw_hold lock: TAKE/GRAB/PICK → ERR HOLD; OBS…O and STBY → CLAWKEEP.
  - Open_Us() = CLWOT (else CLWO); only the raw-plate open uses CLWOR.
  - Boot claw = sb_claw.
  - User's values: CLWOR=1900, CLWOT=1450, CLWC=1150 (smaller µs = more closed).
- **STM32 link:** only the first line received while busy is buffered; later lines are dropped.
- **Ring-2 alignment (`_ring2_smooth`, calc mode):** zone_wheels_only (no arm), S/F retry on V slip, relaxed ranges with map check `_body_clear` (pose0 from `_stop_pose`), ring2_off_mm goal offset via the wheel J (`goalp += J[:,1]*F + J[:,0]*S`).
- **Raw "near" (最近那格) mode:**
  - Plate-center wheel alignment (V); grabbing at the nearest slot in task-code order (current).
  - `_raw_ct_grab` → `_raw_grab_fix` (wheel per-grab correction using store 'RAW' 'ch' J; d = -solve(J, e)) → `_raw_wheels_back`.
  - The recheck/redo realigns the plate center if drift > raw_near_redo_mm (4).
- **Keepalive:** lift bob `_lift_bob` (keepalive_lift_mm=4; goes down if near LFMAX) instead of the ID1 wiggle.
- **Sim:** SimWorld plate_jitter_deg (stop-angle jitter), enc_now plus LIFT SYNC, grab tolerance of 8mm.

3. Files and Code Sections:

**arm_patch/Core/Src/arm.c**

All of the following is committed.

*Lift:*
- `#define LIFT_TOP_MM 130.0f`; `static float g_lchk = 1.5f;` (`LFCHK`).
- LFMAX tun range uses `LIFT_TOP_MM`.
- `static uint8_t lift_nochk = 0;` and `static uint8_t lift_guess = 0;` (declared after lift_boot_how).
- `Lift_Check(float mm)`:
  - returns early if `g_lchk<=0 || !lift_cal_ok || lift_nochk`;
  - wait loop uses `Delay_Report(80)` and `if (car_abort || !Lift_EncPos(...)) return;`;
  - then:
    ```c
    if (lift_guess && Absf(z - mm) <= 20.0f) { /* print "LIFTSYNC %s (guessed %s)" */ if (Absf(z-mm)>=g_lchk){Emm_V5_Stop_Now(LIFT_ADDR,false);HAL_Delay(5);} lift_mm = z; lift_guess = 0; lift_boot_how = "SYNC"; return; }
    if (Absf(z - mm) < g_lchk) return;
    ... "LIFTSLIP want=%s got=%s%s" ...
    if (Absf(z-mm) <= 15.0f) { Emm_V5_Stop_Now(LIFT_ADDR,false); HAL_Delay(5); lift_mm = z; }
    ```
- `Lift_Cal_Do` (renamed). `lift_known=1; lift_mm=z0; lift_guess=0;` now come AFTER the ERR NOENC check.
- Wrapper `Lift_Cal` sets `lift_nochk` around `Lift_Cal_Do`.
- `Lift_Rebase0()`: on LIFT ZERO, if the encoder says ≥0.3mm, rebase lift_enc0, Cal_Save, Pos_Save(0), print `LIFTZERO ENC x -> 0`.
- Lift_Boot: `lift_guess = 1;`, loop `k < 40`; on success `lift_guess = 0;`.
- `LIFT SYNC` command (inside `strcmp(v,"LIFT")==0 && n==2`, before ENC?): Lift_EncPos(lift_mm) → print `LIFTSYNC %s (was %s)`, set lift_mm/known, BOOT=SYNC if guessed, Pos_Save. Errors: ERR NOCAL / ERR NOENC.

*Claw:*
```c
static uint32_t claw_now  = CLAW_BOOT_US;
static uint8_t  claw_hold = 0;
static void Claw_Move(uint32_t target, const char *why) { ... if (target != claw_now) { snprintf(m,..,"CLAWSET %lu %s\r\n",...); Say(m);} ... }
static uint32_t Open_Us(void) { return (uint32_t)((g_clwot >= 500.0f) ? g_clwot : g_clwo); }
static void Claw_O(void) { Claw_Move(Open_Us(), "O"); }
static void Claw_OAt(uint8_t where) { if (where == CLAW_AT_RAW && g_clwor >= 500.0f) { Claw_Move((uint32_t)g_clwor, "OR"); return; } Claw_Move(Open_Us(), (where == CLAW_AT_RAW) ? "OR" : "OT"); }
static void Claw_C(void) { Claw_Move((uint32_t)g_clwc, "C"); }
```
- Seq_Obs: `if (claw_hold) Say("CLAWKEEP OBS\r\n"); else if (ring) Claw_O(); else Claw_OAt(CLAW_AT_RAW);`
- Seq_PickHere / Seq_Take: `if (claw_hold) return "ERR HOLD";`; set `claw_hold = 1` after Claw_C. PickHere sets `claw_hold = 0` after the tray release.
- Seq_Drop: `claw_hold = 0` after Claw_O.
- Seq_Standby: `if (claw_hold) Say("CLAWKEEP STBY\r\n"); else Claw_Move(sb_claw, "STBY");`
- CLAW cmd: `<us>` → `Claw_Move(us,"US")`; anything except C sets `claw_hold=0`.
- Arm_Init: `Sb_Load(); claw_now = (sb_ok && sb_claw >= 500u && sb_claw <= 3000u) ? sb_claw : CLAW_BOOT_US; claw_hold = 0; __HAL_TIM_SET_COMPARE(&htim2, TIM_CHANNEL_3, claw_now);`

**arm_patch/pi/lidar_map_north/mission_hooks.py** (committed; key additions)

*DEFAULTS:*
- `zone_wheels_only=True`, `zone_wheels_moves=8`, `zone_wheels_s_mm=150.0`, `zone_wheels_f_mm=150.0`;
- `ring2_off_mm=dict(F=0.0, S=0.0)`, `ring2_off_max_mm=20.0`;

- `BOOT_TEXT['SYNC']`.

*`_ring2_smooth`:*
- `wonly`; `pose0`/`obs` computed before lo/hi.
- `if wonly and pose0 is not None:` relax scap/fmax.
- `goalp += self._ring2_off_px(J0)`.
- `calc_n` = zone_wheels_moves.
- No arm "small" handoff when wonly.
- Slip: `if wonly and went: note['no_v']=True; ...改用 S/F 再挪一次; a=a_new; continue`, then one more retry.
- `tail`: when wonly, `out['ok']=em<=acc`, no arm; s_range uses `sm_ = scap if (wonly and pose0)`.

*Other functions:*
- `ring2_off()`, `_ring2_off_px(J)`.
- `_recover`: on 'ERR HOLD', sets `maybe_holding` and `disabled`.
- Init: if BOOT=NOENC, request `LIFT SYNC`; ok → re-read the state; 'NOENC' in reply → `disabled` with message "...本轮不夹不放...".
- `_lift_bob(why)` (up keepalive_lift_mm, or down if near LFMAX). `keepalive()` and `_raw_ct_keepalive()` use it.
- `_raw_ct_grab(self, item, k, a, tag, t_end=None, near=False)`: AP → raw_ct_check (reject if > raw_slot_accept_mm 25) → `moved = self._raw_grab_fix(...)` if near → grab_here → finally `_raw_wheels_back(moved)`.
- `_raw_grab_fix(item, tag, t_end)`:
  - median of 2 `material_errors` frames; returns if em ≤ tol;
  - `d = -solve(J, e)`; refuses if |d| > raw_grab_fix_max_mm; refuses if there isn't time before t_end;
  - `_vel_goto(s0+d0, f0+d1, tol 0.6, vmax 25, max_s)`, else `act.chassis_move`;
  - accumulates `total`; `sleep(0.1)`.
- `_raw_wheels_back(moved)`.
- Call site in the near loop: `fix_s` computed; `self._raw_ct_grab(item, k, reach[k], tag, t_end=t_stop + self._raw_ct_stop_len() - T - margin, near=bool(near_k) and left >= need + fix_s)`.

*Near loop (current code, starts at line ~4337, to be changed):*

```python
seen = set(); recheck = False; after = True; cyc = ...; last_stop = None
for i, item in enumerate(todo, 1):
    ... t_end = self.now() + float(cfg.get('raw_track_s', 40.0)); misses, got = 0, False
    while not got and self.now() < t_end:
        focus = ...; ob = self._raw_ct_watch(watch, ..., after_move=after, focus=focus, centers=cen) ...
        if recheck and cen: recheck=False; r_ = self._raw_near_redo(D, comp, median(cen[-3:])); if r_: off, P = r_; continue
        ... occ = occupancy(px) ...
        if near_k: at = occ.get(near_k); names=...; k = near_k if at == int(item.color) else None
        if k is None: misses += 1; log '要的是 X，等它转过来'; skip after raw_ct_skip_stops; continue
        ... left = t_stop + self._raw_ct_stop_len() - self.now(); need = t_go(k) + T + margin; fix_s = ...
        if left < need: wait
        log '直接下爪，放 N 号槽'; got = self._raw_ct_grab(...)
        if got: stats.grab(True); in_tray[item.slot] = item
        near_k: home, comp = self._raw_ct_pose(D); self._ct_home = home
        if near_k and raw_near_recheck ... : recheck = True
```

4. Errors and fixes:
- 16384-encoder test failed: Lift_Check ran during LIFT CAL using the stale calibration. Fixed with `lift_nochk` and the Lift_Cal wrapper.
- Pi lift_slip test: the hint direction for the bottom case was wrong in the test expectation (got > want vs got < want). The test was corrected.
- Wheels-only change broke 3 sim tests that expected an arm handoff:
  - rewrote them (S/F retry test; legacy test uses `zone_wheels_only=False`);
  - the polar geometry test uses `zone_wheels_only=False`.
- Far-park test:
  - the 110mm along-row offset caused ring misidentification, so the scenario was changed to a 100mm lateral offset;
  - the relaxed range needs `pose0`, so the test got `raw={'stops': {'ROUGH': [300,300,0]}}` (the field-center pose collided in the map check);
  - `_make` got a `raw` param.
- The hold lock broke old tests that ran consecutive TAKEs without a release; inserted `run("CLAW O")`.
- DROP test: ZPLC is above the APZ height, so the APZ height was changed to 100.
- The sim LIFT handler crashed on `LIFT SYNC` (float parse); added the handler and `enc_now`.
- Two-angle rule broke old arm_calib/claw tests; updated the expectations (1940, CLAW 1700).
- Ring2 offset test with 35mm (clamped to 20) fell off the ring in the sim; the test now only checks the clamp message.
- Keepalive tests expected the AD wiggle; the old test now runs with `keepalive_lift_mm=0`, and a lift-bob test was added.
- **User corrections:**
  - "限制速度没必要啊和这个没关系纯是因为这个爪子莫名奇妙松开啊" → dropped HLDSPD.
  - The user rejected arm correction for ring alignment.
  - The user wants immediate grabbing rather than waiting.

5. Problem Solving:
- Proven via the CLAWSET logs that the STM32 sends the close (1150) at the bottom on every GRAB; the lift boot was POS (encoder-based).
- Raw grab misses are attributed to:
  - material position errors of 10–16mm at the nearest slot (plate stop-angle variance);
  - the 15mm fix cap refusing corrections;
  - likely a stale camera frame after the wheel move (no settle), so BLUE's re-measure showed 9.3mm.
- Waiting time comes from task-order grabbing.
- Move-back + realign ("车被带偏了 4.1mm") wastes time.
- Possible claw servo hardware/overheat (CLWC too tight) was advised to the user, but not confirmed.

7. Pending Tasks:
Implement in mission_hooks.py (raw near mode), per the plan already announced to the user:
1. **Any-order grabbing** (new cfg, e.g. `raw_near_any_order=True`): at each stop, if the color at the nearest slot belongs to any remaining needed item of this batch, grab that item immediately (into its own tray slot) instead of waiting for task-code order.
2. **Per-grab fix improvements:**
   - after each wheel move, sleep a settle time (e.g. `raw_grab_fix_settle_s=0.3`) before measuring; median of 2 frames;
   - `raw_grab_fix_max_mm` 15 → 30;
   - `raw_grab_fix_moves` 2 → 3;
   - if the image didn't move after a V move, retry with S/F (`chassis_move`);
   - respect the t_end time budget.
3. **No move-back after the grab** (e.g. `raw_grab_fix_back=False`):
   - when the grab fix is on, raise the plate-center redo threshold to max(raw_near_redo_mm, raw_grab_fix_max_mm) so it doesn't realign every time;
   - clamp the cumulative offset from the aligned position (≤ ~30mm).

### 助手

这一步（第 13 步"原料盘上方的姿态"）只能调手臂角度，所以输入 `CLAW 1600` 会提示"看不懂"。原料盘上方夹爪张多大，是后面单独一步 **CLWOR** 调的，现在的值就是刚才那个 1900。数越小，爪子张得越小。

**做法一：接着往下走**

1. 这一步姿态满意的话，直接按回车保存。不想改姿态就输入 `s` 跳过。
2. 一直走到标题是"夹爪在原料盘上方张开 CLWOR(夹原料之前)"的那一步，在提示符后输入：

```
1600
```

不带符号的数就是直接转到这个值。想在现在的基础上减，也可以输入：

```
-100
```

3. 看爪子张开的大小合适了，按回车保存。

**做法二：直接跳到这一步**

先输入 `q` 退出，再运行：

```
python3 arm_calib.py --from CLWOR
```

调好按回车保存。后面的步骤不想动，就输入 `q` 退出，已经保存的值都会留着。

注意：
- CLWOR 必须比夹紧的 CLWC（你现在是 1150）大不少。爪子张开后还要能套下物料最粗的底面（直径 50mm），不然下爪会压到物料。
- 命令里的减号一定要用英文的 `-`，不要用中文的"－"或"—"。

你刚才那段日志用的还是上一版程序（日志里写的是"按任务码顺序"），所以下面这些改动都还没上车。

**改了什么**
1. **对准以后车不动。** 车轮只对准一次原料盘中心，之后不再按物料位置挪车。日志里会记一行"物料离爪子点多少 mm、在爪子前/后/左/右"，方便看是不是整体偏了。
2. **对准后马上夹。**
   - 这一格停的是这一批要的哪个颜色，就马上夹哪个，放进它自己的槽，不再按任务码顺序等。
   - 如果对准的时候原料盘已经停下了，这一次停下就直接夹，不再等它转一下。
   - 仿真里原料区平均用时从 108 秒降到 86 秒。
   - ★ 比赛规则按任务码顺序算"正确抓取"，每个 2 分，不按顺序夹可能丢这部分分。比赛想按顺序夹，就在配置里把 `raw_near_any_order` 改成 `false`。
3. **没夹上会再夹一次。** 夹过的颜色如果又出现在原料盘上，就把抓取数减回去，等它转过来再夹一次。所以屏上的抓取数不会再把没夹上的算进去。
   - 有一个漏洞：画面多半只看得到最近那一格，最后一个如果没夹上又已经转走了，看不出来。
4. **改了手臂姿态不再乱走。** 你在 arm_calib 里把 A1G/A2E 改成了 55.5 / -946.4，和示教时对不上。上一版会改用老办法开着车到处找圆心，这就是第二次乱动、偏 40mm 卡住的原因。现在会直接停下，提示你重新示教。

**更新步骤**（命令里的减号都要用英文的 `-`）

1. 把 zip 解压到 `下载\raw_1021i\`，然后在电脑上运行：

2. 在 run_live 里输入 `q` 退出，再运行：

```
rteach RAW near 1
```
4. 再测：
```
mtest RAW 1 force
```

**"下爪了但是不夹取"要你在车上看一下**

每次下爪，程序都发了夹紧命令（日志里的"1150µs 夹紧"），固件里的顺序也没问题：下降、合上、等一会儿、再抬起。所以剩下的原因只可能在车上：
- **爪子有没有合上：** 到了下面爪子根本没合上，就是舵机或供电的问题。
- **认物料的偏差：** 你这次把原料盘上方的张开 CLWOR 从 1900 改成了 1700，张得更小了，物料偏 12~16mm 时更容易夹不住。
- **高度：** 可能 ZGRAB=85 的高度不对，爪子在物料上面就合上了。
- **蓝色认错：** 日志里 BLUE 一次被认成离爪子 122mm、一次 60mm，很可能是把蓝色爪子当成了物料。

下次测试请看清是哪一种：爪子没合上、合上了但没夹到物料、还是夹偏了。再把日志发给我。

这个示教是干嘛用的


### 助手

`rteach RAW near` 是告诉程序：爪子正好在原料盘最近那一格正上方时，摄像头里的原料盘长什么样。

**示教时做的事：**
1. 手臂摆到看原料盘的姿态（就是 arm_calib 里调的 A1G/A2E）。
2. 车轮慢慢挪，把最近那一格上的物料对到画面里的爪子点下面。
3. 记下这时原料盘中心在画面里的位置，还有手臂角度、车挪了多少，存进 `ring_teach.json`。

**比赛时怎么用：**
- 车停到原料区，手臂摆到同样的姿态。
- 车轮挪到画面里原料盘中心和示教时在同一个位置，这时爪子就又在最近那一格正上方了。
- 之后车不再动，物料转到这一格就直接下爪。

**为什么现在要重新做：**
记下的位置是按当时的手臂姿态算的。你在 arm_calib 里改了 A1G/A2E（现在是 55.5 / -946.4），摄像头的视角跟着变了，旧的记录就对不上。拿旧记录对准，爪子就不在那一格上方，会夹偏。所以程序发现对不上时会直接停下，不再开着车乱找。

以后只要改了 A1G/A2E，或者摄像头、爪子被碰过，都要重新示教一次。

**示教前准备：**
- 车按比赛时的位置停在原料盘前面。
- 原料盘停住不转。
- 最近那一格上放一个红色或黄色物料（不要用蓝色，容易和蓝色爪子混在一起认错）。

然后输入下面的命令，最后的数字是这个物料的颜色号（1 红、2 黄）：
```
rteach RAW near 1
```

  程序会自己下去夹一下把物料夹正，再测出位置。

**2. 上面弄好以后再示教**
```
rteach RAW near 1
```
这时物料离爪子点应该只有几毫米到一两厘米，车只会稍微挪一点。

## [2026-10-21 12:32 UTC] 用户

cd ~/lidar_map_north
python3 arm_calib.py这整个流程是不是出问题了这里绝对有东西出问题了这个夹取的过程有时候爪子不夹取有时候就是下降的时候不夹取抬起来了再夹取


### 助手

先说结论：arm_calib 这个流程本身没问题。按你说的现象，问题多半在夹爪舵机或者它的供电上。

**为什么这么判断**

你之前发的每次 `mtest RAW` 日志里，下爪时都打印了"1150µs 夹紧 ← GRAB"。这说明 STM32 在爪子到了下面就发了夹紧命令。

固件里下爪的顺序我又核对了一遍：
1. 升降降到 ZGRAB，用编码器确认已经到了。
2. 发夹紧（脉宽一下子变到 1150）。
3. 等 CLWAIT（默认 400 毫秒）。
4. 再抬起来。

命令是在抬起之前发的。如果爪子要等抬起来以后才合上，说明舵机收到命令后反应慢了，或者那一下根本没动。常见原因：
- 舵机供电不够。升降、转盘、手臂同时在动，电压会被拉低。
- 舵机发热进了保护。
- 舵机线或插头接触不好。
- CLWAIT 设得太短，舵机还没合到位升降就起来了。

**下面几个测试帮我确认是哪一个**（都在 run_live 里输入）

1. 看等待时间是多少：
```
get CLWAIT
```

2. 只动爪子，看反应快不快、每次都动不动。下面两条交替输入五六次：
```
arm CLAW C
```
```
arm CLAW 1700
```
- 每次都立刻动：舵机本身没问题，接着做第 3 步。
- 有时不动或者慢半拍：是舵机、供电或接线的问题。摸一下舵机烫不烫，检查插头，看舵机是不是和升降、转盘共用一路电源。

3. 把等待时间加长，再测一次夹取：
```
set CLWAIT 800
```
```
mtest RAW 1 force
```
如果这样就每次都在下面合上了，说明是舵机慢。之后可以在 arm_calib 里把这个值存下来。

4. 爪子合上的时候用手轻轻挡一下手指，感觉力气大不大。没什么力气基本就是供电不够。

请把第 1 步的数值、第 2 步看到的情况、第 3 步的日志发给我。

还有一件事：你上次示教时物料离爪子点 115.7mm，车才会追着开出去。示教前请先输入 `arm OBS RAW O`，确认爪子真的在最近那格物料的正上方。

### 助手

I went through these parameters. Nothing here explains the claw closing late:

- **CLWAIT = 400:** after the close command the arm waits 0.4 s before lifting, and the claw only travels from 1700 to 1150. A healthy servo closes in about 0.15 s, so 400 is enough.
- **CLSPD = 0:** the close is not slowed; the pulse jumps straight to 1150.
- **Lift:** ZOBRAW 116 → ZGRAB 85 is 31mm, about 0.3 s down plus the 200ms wait (LFMRG). The firmware also checks with the encoder that the lift has arrived before closing. The logs had no LIFTSLIP, so the claw really is at the bottom when it gets the close command.

So "下降的时候不夹取，抬起来了再夹取" means the servo has already received the close command but moves late. That points to the servo itself: power, heat, or wiring. Please run these two tests in run_live (use the English `-` in commands):

1. Claw on its own. Enter these two alternately, five or six times, and watch whether the claw responds immediately every time:
```
arm CLAW C
```
```
arm CLAW 1700
```
If it sometimes doesn't move or responds late: check the servo plug and wiring, feel whether the servo is hot, and check whether it shares one power line with the lift and the tray turntable. If it does, the voltage drops when they move at the same time.

2. Wait longer, then test grabbing:
```
set CLWAIT 800
```
```
mtest RAW 1 force
```
If it now always closes at the bottom, the servo is slow or underpowered. Keep 800 for now, and fix the power or replace the servo when you can.

The TEMP "recognized but didn't place" problem is separate: ring 2 was 46mm off and needed the car to back up 43mm. The program checks the map assuming the car is exactly at the planned stop point, so it allowed only 30mm. Ring 2 was still 16mm off, over the 10mm limit, so nothing was placed.

I was about to change it so moves within 60mm of the stop point skip the map check, but you interrupted before the change was written. Should I go ahead with it?

The map-check change I asked you about did get written to the file, even though that command was cancelled. You haven't said yes yet, so I'm reverting it rather than committing:

工作区已经恢复干净。那段代码是我在你点"取消"之前已经写进文件的：暂存区（TEMP）离停车点 60mm 以内挪车时不按地图查。你还没同意，所以我把它撤掉了，没有提交。

两个分支上保存的还是上一版（d85d021），也就是你手上那个 `raw_1021i.zip` 里的 `mission_hooks.py`。这次没有新的提交，也没有新的包。

暂存区 2 号环那个问题（要后退 43mm，地图查只让退 30mm，结果没放）要不要按这个办法改，你说一声"改"，我就改好、跑完测试、提交并打包发给你。

现在这两个区域的爪子都不夹取你好好检查一下是什么原因要是没做完一个半小时后设置个定时任务把所有的东西检查一遍，问题1就是这个爪子的问题必须解决掉为什么不夹取的问题，问题二就是你把整个代码检查一边有没有bug，第三就是你把所有的东西在这个github的库里面备份一份知道了吗，还有就是这个小车运动速度的问题运动太慢了得加速一下还有就是这个在暂存区域的这个问题，故意对偏的问题，把任务列一下你能理解我的意思吗


### 助手

我的理解是下面这几件事，你确认一下：

**任务列表**

1. **爪子不夹取（原料区、暂存区都不夹）：必须找到原因并解决。**
   - 从头查固件：GRAB（原料盘上夹）、PICK（地上取回）、TAKE（从车上转盘取）这几段流程的顺序和等待时间；我加的夹爪锁（ERR HOLD/CLAWKEEP）有没有挡住夹紧；脉宽有没有被限幅截掉。
   - 再查参数：CLWC、CLWOR、CLWOT、CLWAIT、ZGRAB，以及暂存区取回的高度 ZPLC（你现在是 0，要核对）。
   - 还要对照树莓派发出的命令和日志。
   - 软件上的问题我直接改。是舵机、供电这类硬件问题，我给你几条一看就能分清的测试命令。
2. **把整个代码检查一遍找 bug。** 固件 arm.c/main.c/chassis.c、树莓派的 mission_hooks、arm_link、arm_calib 等都查。查到的改掉，跑完全部测试。
3. **备份到 GitHub。** 代码、文档、接手说明、对话记录都提交推送，再打一个备份标记，以后能回到这个版本。
   - 只在车上的标定文件（map_config、servo_cal、vision_cal、place_adj、ring_teach、claw_mask 这些）我这边拿不到。要备份的话，我给你一条打包命令，你把包发给我，我放进仓库里单独的备份文件夹（以后的更新包不会带它们，也不会覆盖车上的）。
4. **小车运动太慢，要加速。** 默认理解为：停车点之间走路线的速度，以及对准时车轮慢慢挪的速度，都提上去，同时保证停得准。手臂和升降要不要也加快，你告诉我。
5. **暂存区放不下去的问题。** 上次 2 号环偏了 46mm，按地图检查只让后退 30mm，还差 16mm 就没放。就是我上次问你的那个改法：离停车点 60mm 以内挪车时不按地图限制。
6. **故意对偏（r2off）的问题。** 检查暂存区、粗加工区对准 2 号环时故意对偏有没有真的起作用，日志里写清楚偏了多少，调起来方便。这个具体哪里不对，你补一句最好。
7. **定时任务。** 开始干活时我设一个 1 个半小时后的定时任务。到时候没做完，就把所有东西再检查一遍，接着做。

全部做完后，我把要传的文件打包，写好传输、烧录步骤发给你。

这样理解对吗？你回"对"我就开始。第 4 条（速度包括哪些）和第 6 条（对偏哪里不对）有要补的，一起告诉我。

  **Firmware review: claw/lift timing and bookkeeping (read-only)**
  
  The tests pass (arm 310/310, chassis 222/222, servo 41/41). The `TT_WaitReady()` before `Claw_C()` (arm.c:1533) is present. I confirmed findings 1–6 by running the real arm.c against the repo's test stubs in a scratchpad harness (`/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-car111/1fa2cdb1-d71d-5819-8ee9-f97d7689ec3f/scratchpad/probe/probe.c`, `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-car111/1fa2cdb1-d71d-5819-8ee9-f97d7689ec3f/scratchpad/probe/probe2.c`). No repo files were changed.
  
  Overall: in every GRAB/TAKE/DROP sequence, the firmware only sends the claw command after Lift_Goto has finished, the encoder check has run, and TT_WaitReady has returned. So "CLAWSET 1150 C" is printed at the right moment. I see two firmware paths that make the claw close at the wrong *height*: findings 1 and 2.
  
  ### Confirmed bugs (ranked)
  
  1. **The lift slip check can report a fake slip and overwrite the height with a value 40 mm wrong. GRAB then closes the claw in mid-air.** arm.c:646, 675-685 (search window set in Lift_EncPos, 606-630).
     - What's wrong: Lift_Check always searches the encoder within ±20 mm of the target. If the lift falls short by 25–55 mm, the reading aliases to a small "slip". Examples: the driver is in stall protection, a frame is lost, or the carriage is blocked.
     - Scenario: probe S2 runs GRAB from ZOBRAW = 116 to ZGRAB = 85 with the lift not moving. The firmware prints `LIFTSLIP want=85 got=76`, sets lift_mm = 76 while the lift is still at 116, closes the claw at 116, and carries on. The next `LIFT 116` drives 40 mm into the top stop. The ">15 mm only report" guard never fires.
     - Also: the "too far, kept" path still lets the sequence continue to Claw_C/Claw_O.
     - Fix:
       - Pass the move's start height into Lift_Check.
       - Correct only if z lies between start and target (±2 mm).
       - Otherwise, and whenever |z−mm| > 15, return an error so the sequence aborts with `ERR LIFT` before the claw moves.
  
  2. **`LIFT ZERO` with the encoder read from the field's 0x36 source makes the boot height wrong after the next power cycle.** arm.c:2547, plus Lift_Rebase0 at 1009-1029.
     - What's wrong: in the field, 0x31 always reads 0, so the firmware reads 0x36, the driver's real-time position. `Emm_V5_Reset_CurPos_To_Zero` zeroes that same counter. Rebase0 then saves an encoder zero that is only valid until the lift driver loses power.
     - Scenario: probe S1. `LIFT ZERO` replies `LIFTZERO ENC 32.47 -> 0`, which is a telltale. After a power cycle, boot reports `LIFTBOOT 67.5` with the lift really at 60. Every later height is 7.5 mm off; it can be up to ±20 mm. Lift_Check agrees with the wrong frame, so it never flags this. ZGRAB then lands above the material.
     - The Pi tells the user to run `LIFT ZERO` after every lost height. `LIFT HOME` (2553-2566) never rebases at all.
     - Fix:
       - Don't send Reset_CurPos_To_Zero when lift_cal_ok. All moves are relative, so nothing reads the driver's zero.
       - Call Lift_Rebase0 after HOME.
       - Redo `LIFT CAL` right after a fresh power-up.
     - Assumption: the driver's 0x36 counter starts from the absolute angle at power-up, which is what Lift_Boot already relies on. One bench check confirms it.
  
  3. **`TT <µs>` leaves tt_slot stale, which bypasses the new TTWAIT guard.** arm.c:2517-2521, 1264.
     - What's wrong: arm_calib.py (lines 360 and 427) sends `TT <µs>`. A later `TT_Go` to the same slot index neither waits nor arms tt_ready_at.
     - Scenario: probe S3. The claw closed 515 ms after the turntable started moving, with TTWAIT = 2000. The tray release has the same problem.
     - Fix: set `tt_slot = 0` in the `TT <µs>` branch and at boot (2374).
  
  4. **`LIFT SYNC` after an aborted long move picks the wrong revolution.** arm.c:2576.
     - What's wrong: when the aborted move spans more than 36 mm, the abort path sets lift_known = 0 but keeps the start height. SYNC searches ±20 mm around that stale start.
     - Scenario: probe S4. `!` during PARK (115→60) stops the lift at 85. SYNC answers `LIFTSYNC 125` and sets lift_known = 1, a 40 mm error.
     - Fix: remember the aborted move's start and target. If the span is over 40 mm, refuse SYNC; otherwise center the search on the midpoint.
  
  5. **NaN passes every range check.** arm.c:248, ParseF at 2449, AD at 2693; main.c:850 and Servo_Clamp at 1103.
     - What's wrong: strtod accepts "nan", and every `<`/`>` range test is false for NaN. Probe S6: `SET LFMAX nan` is accepted, which removes the lift's top clamp. `AD/AF/AP/APN` would send an unclamped NaN target to the bus servo.
     - Trigger: the Pi formats any NaN from its vision math as "nan".
     - Fix: reject non-finite values with `!(v >= lo && v <= hi)` and `isfinite()`.
  
  6. **Boot erases the position log without writing a fresh record.** arm.c:1095-1098.
     - What's wrong: if the standby move doesn't move the lift (lift already at sb_z = the compiled ZHI default of 100), the log stays empty.
     - Scenario: probe S5. A boot with no lift motion, then a power-off, then the next boot aliases 100 to 60. The firmware then drives to 140.
     - Fix: call `Pos_Save(lift_mm)` after that Cal_Save.
  
  7. **Servo_Wait never checks car_abort.** main.c:1241.
     - What's wrong: `!` can't stop Servo_Move. That covers Stages, Leave_Tray's LVDEG turn, Seq_Drop's retract, and AF/AD, each for up to 9 s.
     - Fix: in the loop, `if (car_abort) { Servo_Hold(id, a); return 0; }`.
  
  8. **Lift_Check blocks inside Move_Over's 15 ms servo loop.** Via Lift_Done_Now, arm.c:429 and 1325.
     - What's wrong: whenever the lift ends ≥0.3 mm off target, polling stops for ≥85 ms, and up to about 1 s if the lift arrives late. ID1 runs on toward its target plus S1COMP (5°), so the arm settles up to 5° past A1D.
     - Fix: defer the check until after the loop, in Lift_Finish.
  
  9. **An overlong UART line runs its tail as a separate command.** main.c:654-661.
     - What's wrong: after an overflow, link_len resets but bytes keep accumulating. On `\n`, the remainder is executed as a command. A `!` inside SCR text also triggers an E-stop.
     - Fix: add a "discard until `\n`" flag.
  
  ### Speculative / needs a bench check
  
  - **The new wait shifts the close time.** With TT_WaitReady at 1533, the claw now closes about 0.80 s after GRAB on every slot change. The Pi's model (mission_hooks.py:1821-1829) assumes about 0.68 s. The rotating plate may restart before the grip. Fix: move the tray turntable earlier, or add max(0, TTWAIT − descent) to the Pi's model.
  - **"Closes after rising" is probably mechanical.** If ZGRAB presses the fingers onto the plate by less than LFCHK (1.5 mm), the closed-loop lift keeps pushing; nothing stops it. The fingers can't close until the lift rises. Fix: Stop_Now whenever the settled error is ≥0.3 mm. Also try a longer CLWAIT.
  - **ZOBRAW = LFMAX = 116** keeps the lift pushing at the top while the camera aligns. That could trip the driver's stall protection, which is only cleared at boot or HOME, and leads straight into finding 1.
  - **An STM32 reset mid-carry** sets the claw to the standby pulse (or 2090 if no standby is saved) and clears claw_hold. That would explain "opened and closed briefly".
  - **Lift_Goto abort race:** if `!` arrives during the 5 ms pre-move delay, Stop_Now is sent back-to-back with Pos_Control (arm.c:501-510).
  - **V mode:** no integration error found. It ignores the driver's acceleration ramp, about 1 mm per start.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
- **Firmware GRAB/PICK (Seq_PickHere):** TT_Go(slot) → open → Lift_Goto(z) → [new] TT_WaitReady → Claw_C → WAIT(CLWAIT) → Go_Tray → TT_WaitReady → release (CLWOT) → lift.
  - TAKE already waits for TT before lowering.
- **Lift encoder:** 40mm per revolution, so heights alias by 40mm.
  - Lift_EncPos searches ±20mm around `near`.
  - New span check: trust z only inside the heights travelled since the last verification (±2mm) and only if |z−mm| ≤ 15; otherwise `ERR LIFT`.
  - LIFTPUSH: blocked 0.5mm..LFCHK short → Stop_Now (stop pushing; fingers pressed on a surface can't close).
- **Move_Over** (OVLAP=1) polls servos every 15ms. Lift_Check inside Lift_Done_Now used to block up to 1 s, so ID1 overshot (S1COMP 5°). Fix: defer the check to Lift_Finish.
- **V velocity mode:**
  - STM32 integrates the commanded (rounded integer rpm) speed; 1 rpm ≈ 4.1mm/s forward, 3.9mm/s strafe.
  - Serial round trip ~0.1–0.2 s.
  - VEL_TIMEOUT 400ms.
  - The new `_vel_goto` predicts the coast (`vcmd·lat`), uses timed 1-rpm pulses per axis, and only reports arrived after stopping.
- **Ring 2 calc alignment:** `fit_j` refits the wheel↔image J only from moves ≥8mm. The r2off goal is recomputed after a refit.
- **Raw nearest-slot mode:**
  - any-order;
  - known_stop from alignment frames (material angles around the rim center);
  - regrab;
  - leftover check;
  - `_raw_plate_still` cross-check;
  - grab timing T = max(lift descent, TTWAIT) + close.
- **Sim:** `vel_latency_s`, `vel_quant`, `plate_jitter_deg`, `plate_stop_s` overridable via plate_world kw. The sim GRAB doesn't emit CLAWSET lines and doesn't model the TT wait.

4. Errors and fixes:
- **Test TTWAIT check passed on the old firmware:** the descent was longer than 800ms in the test. Fixed with `Arm_Param_Set("TTWAIT", 4000)`. `run("SET …")` isn't valid; params go through Arm_Param_Set.
- **`_vel_goto` oscillation (real 9 s stall):** fixed with the predictive rewrite. At 0.3 s latency a 0.7mm move hunts, so the tolerance was relaxed after pulses.
- **test_moving_the_car_after_ring2_forces_realign seed 4:** caused by fit_j learning from 1mm moves. Fixed with the ≥8mm filter.
- **test_center_bad_stored_wheel_j:** broke with raw_center_speed 20, so it was kept at 15.
- **Duplicated early return in Lift_Finish:** fixed, and the deferred check placed there.
- **test_arm.c compile error:** my replacement swallowed a comment opener; restored.
- **Dead-lift test left phys/lift mismatch:** this broke later TAKE tests; fixed by setting `phys_mm = 69.0`.
- **The user rejected tool calls twice** (a full test run; the zone_map_free edit + test run) and interrupted. I reverted the unapproved edit, then waited for approval.
